## CFG01 accepted — almaidan source configuration, 26 October 2026 Kuwait

**ALMAIDAN-CFG01 is complete WITH LIMITS. PRO-259 remains In Progress/Hold; R6 STOP.** [Immutable acceptance](evidence/ALMAIDAN-CFG01-SOURCE-ACCEPTANCE-20261026.json), SHA256 `35f7627e018d7aa249eb5cef9cff3bbef2cdd2cb4868942265f65a377a7bf699`, binds the exact R1 source, both final Database/Security concurrence reports and independent Root verification.

The actual default server configuration loader now requires supplied PostgreSQL URLs to name canonical `almaidan`; validates supplied DIRECT_URL; rejects path/identity overrides, raw Unicode whitespace/control and malformed percent escapes; and emits fixed errors without credentials. Original Firebase/namespace/mock/payment guards, absence semantics and configuration interface are preserved. Server-only example URLs remain blank. Direct URL omission remains optional only at the application runtime boundary, not a claim of CLI readiness.

Current verification: all five actual configuration test groups pass; full-server and focused strict TypeScript checks exit0; Root independently reproduces both URL groups2/2 with all68 tracked identities unchanged. Both final reviews concur WITH LIMITS and all scoped findings are closed. Original three guard groups are byte-equivalent. Compatible prior SRC-CLOSE/CONTENT01 results retain their original scope; no replay or duplicate test credit. Fourteen R0 copies are preserved. The initial R0 failed run's overwritten raw log remains unavailable; it is recorded honestly and not reconstructed.

**No physical database was created, renamed, removed or migrated. Firestore is unchanged.** Logical URL/name validation cannot establish a physical target/operator, pooled/direct identity, schema/grants, installed policy/issuer custody, native/material fit or runtime readiness. Existing PRO-259 gates remain open. Next defined source stage is ENG02: reviewed PostgreSQL ownership, both-engine persistence/realtime/admission migration design, then ENG03 source/parity and separately admitted ENG04 qualification/cutover. The [adopted staged plan](../output/pro259-almaidan-20261025/ALMAIDAN-PLAN.md) preserves old data until a verified replacement and owner-authorized cutover. No following part or R6 operation starts automatically. Earlier checkpoints below remain historical.
